> 379	            node_id: body.node_id,
> 380	            level,
> 381	          },
> 382	        });
> 383	
> 384	        await admin
> 385	          .from("infra_health_logs")
> 386	          .insert({ service: "canvas-execute", status: "healthy" })
> 387	          .then(() => {}, (e: unknown) => console.error("[canvas-execute] health log failed:", e));
> 388	
> 389	        return new Response(
> 390	          JSON.stringify({
> 391	            execution_id: execution.id,
> 392	            status: "success",
> 393	            enhanced_prompt: enhancedPrompt,
> 394	            credits_charged: creditCost,
> 395	          }),
> 396	          { headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } },
> 397	        );
> 398	      } catch (err: any) {
> 399	        console.error("Magic Prompt generation failed:", err);
> 400	        await admin
> 401	          .from("vm_canvas_executions")
> 402	          .update({
> 403	            status: "failed",
> 404	            error_message: err.message || "Failed to generate magic prompt",
> 405	            completed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
> 406	          })
> 407	          .eq("id", execution.id);
> 408	
> 409	        await admin
> 410	          .from("infra_health_logs")
> 411	          .insert({ service: "canvas-execute", status: "unhealthy" })
> 412	          .then(() => {}, (e: unknown) => console.error("[canvas-execute] health log failed:", e));
> 413	
> 414	        return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: err.message || "Failed to generate magic prompt" }), {
> 415	          status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
> 416	        });
> 417	      }
> 418	    }
> 419	
> 420	    // ── Phase 4.2c async video branch ────────────────────────────────
> 421	    // image_to_video: submit to Higgsfield with ?hf_webhook=<callback>,
> 422	    // return immediately with status='queued'. Webhook does atomic deduct
> 423	    // after upload-OK; useCanvasJobsRealtime flips the UI when status moves.
> 424	    if (body.node_type === "image_to_video") {
> 425	      return await submitHiggsfieldVideo({
> 426	        admin,
> 427	        supabaseUrl,
> 428	        apiKey: keys.higgsfield,
> 429	        apiSecret: keys.higgsfield_secret,
> 430	        body,
> 431	        execution,
> 432	        corsHeaders,
> 433	      });
> 434	    }
> 435	
> 436	    // ── Route to provider ────────────────────────────────────────────
> 437	    let outputUrl = "";
> 438	    let outputB64 = "";
> 439	    let error = "";
> 440	
> 441	    try {
> 442	      if (body.provider === "openai") {
> 443	        const result = await generateOpenAI(keys.openai, body);
> 444	        outputUrl = result.url;
> 445	        outputB64 = result.b64;
> 446	      } else if (body.provider === "replicate") {
> 447	        const result = await generateReplicate(keys.replicate, body);
> 448	        outputUrl = result.url;
> 449	      } else if (body.provider === "openrouter") {
> 450	        const result = await generateOpenRouter(keys.openrouter, body);
> 451	        outputUrl = result.url;
> 452	        outputB64 = result.b64;
> 453	      } else if (body.provider === "higgsfield") {
> 454	        const result = await generateHiggsfield(keys.higgsfield, keys.higgsfield_secret, body);
> 455	        outputUrl = result.url;
> 456	      } else {
> 457	        throw new Error(`Unknown provider: ${body.provider}`);
> 458	      }
> 459	    } catch (providerErr: any) {
> 460	      error = providerErr.message || "Provider error";
> 461	      console.error(`Provider ${body.provider} error:`, providerErr);
> 462	    }
> 463	
> 464	    // ── Upload to storage (b64 OR URL response) ──────────────────────
> 465	    // Round 3 hotfix: OpenAI defaults to URL response (no response_format).
> 466	    // OpenRouter chat-completions multimodal returns URL. Both paths must
> 467	    // persist to canvas-assets so the provider URL's short TTL doesn't break
> 468	    // the asset_url stored in vm_canvas_assets. Pattern mirrors rescue-video
> 469	    // (Google Veo) + higgsfield-webhook (DoP video) download+upload flow.
> 470	    let storedUrl = outputUrl;
> 471	    let storedKey: string | null = null;
> 472	    let storedContentType = "image/png";
> 473	    if (!error && (outputB64 || outputUrl)) {
> 474	      try {
> 475	        let bytes: Uint8Array | null = null;
> 476	        let contentType = "image/png";
> 477	
> 478	        if (outputB64) {
> 479	          bytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(outputB64), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
> 480	        } else if (outputUrl) {
> 481	          // Fetch the provider's signed URL, validate, then re-host in bucket.
> 482	          const dlResp = await fetch(outputUrl);
> 483	          if (dlResp.ok) {
> 484	            const ct = dlResp.headers.get("content-type") || "";
> 485	            if (ct.startsWith("image/")) contentType = ct;
> 486	            bytes = new Uint8Array(await dlResp.arrayBuffer());
> 487	          } else {
> 488	            console.error("[canvas-execute] provider URL fetch failed:", dlResp.status);
> 489	          }
> 490	        }
> 491	
> 492	        if (bytes && bytes.byteLength > 0) {
> 493	          const ext = contentType.split("/")[1]?.split(";")[0] || "png";
> 494	          const fileName = `${user.id}/${execution.id}.${ext}`;
> 495	          const { error: uploadErr } = await admin.storage
> 496	            .from("canvas-assets")
> 497	            .upload(fileName, bytes, { contentType, upsert: true });
> 498	          if (!uploadErr) {
> 499	            // canvas-assets is PRIVATE (since bucket creation 2026-05-14 — public-URL shapes
> 500	            // 400; proven 2026-07-03). Sign 7d, parity with higgsfield-webhook.
> 501	            const { data: urlData, error: signErr } = await admin.storage
> 502	              .from("canvas-assets")
> 503	              .createSignedUrl(fileName, 7 * 24 * 60 * 60);
> 504	            if (!signErr && urlData?.signedUrl) {
> 505	              storedUrl = urlData.signedUrl;
> 506	              storedKey = fileName;
> 507	              storedContentType = contentType;
> 508	            } else {
> 509	              console.error("[canvas-execute] sign error:", signErr);
> 510	            }
> 511	          } else {
> 512	            console.error("[canvas-execute] upload error:", uploadErr);
> 513	          }
> 514	        }
> 515	      } catch (uploadErr) {
> 516	        console.error("Upload error:", uploadErr);
> 517	      }
> 518	    }
> 519	
> 520	    // ── Save asset record ────────────────────────────────────────────
> 521	    let assetId: string | null = null;
> 522	    if (!error && storedUrl) {
> 523	      const { data: asset } = await admin
> 524	        .from("vm_canvas_assets")
> 525	        .insert({
> 526	          user_id: user.id,
> 527	          project_id: body.project_id,
> 528	          asset_type: "image",
> 529	          storage_key: storedKey ?? `${user.id}/${execution.id}.png`,
> 530	          storage_bucket: "canvas-assets",
> 531	          public_url: storedUrl,
> 532	          mime_type: storedContentType,
> 533	          provider: body.provider,
> 534	          model_used: body.model,
> 535	          node_type: dbNodeType,
> 536	          prompt: body.prompt,
> 537	          negative_prompt: body.negative_prompt,
> 538	          parameters: body.parameters || {},
> 539	          credits_cost: creditCost,
> 540	          node_id: body.node_id,
> 541	          input_asset_id: resolvedInputAssetId,
> 542	        })
> 543	        .select("id")
> 544	        .single();
> 545	      assetId = asset?.id;
> 546	
> 547	      // Dual-write to the canonical asset registry (creative_assets) so the HyperFrames editor / other modules can
> 548	      // reuse this Canvas output bidirectionally (the "power of the outputs" spine). Fail-soft — never break exec.
> 549	      try {
> 550	        await admin.rpc("register_creative_asset", {
> 551	          p_user_id: user.id,
> 552	          p_kind: "image",
> 553	          p_storage_bucket: "canvas-assets",
> 554	          p_storage_key: storedKey ?? `${user.id}/${execution.id}.png`,
> 555	          p_is_public: false, // canvas-assets is a PRIVATE bucket — display resolves via signed URL (SOP private-bucket-asset-signing). true here minted 83 dead-URL rows.
> 556	          p_source_module: "canvas-studio",
> 557	          p_source_job_id: execution.id,
> 558	          p_mime_type: storedContentType,
> 559	          p_provider: body.provider,
> 560	          p_model: body.model,
> 561	          p_prompt: body.prompt,
> 562	        });
> 563	      } catch (e) {
> 564	        console.error("[canvas-execute] creative_assets register skipped:", e);
> 565	      }
> 566	    }
> 567	
> 568	    // ── Update execution status ──────────────────────────────────────
> 569	    const finalStatus = error ? "failed" : "success";
> 570	    await admin
> 571	      .from("vm_canvas_executions")
> 572	      .update({
> 573	        status: finalStatus,
> 574	        output_url: storedUrl || null,
> 575	        output_asset_id: assetId,
> 576	        error_message: error || null,
> 577	        completed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
> 578	      })
> 579	      .eq("id", execution.id);
> 580	
> 581	    // ── SSP-01 OE03 Pillar 4 (Observability) ─────────────────────────
> 582	    // Best-effort health pulse; failure to log MUST NOT block the response.
> 583	    await admin
> 584	      .from("infra_health_logs")
> 585	      .insert({ service: "canvas-execute", status: error ? "unhealthy" : "healthy" })
> 586	      .then(() => {}, (e: unknown) => console.error("[canvas-execute] health log failed:", e));
> 587	
> 588	    // ── Deduct credits on success ────────────────────────────────────
> 589	    // SSP-01 OE04: action tagging is REQUIRED for the daily cap query to work.
> 590	    // canvas_image_spend / canvas_video_spend are the canonical action tags.
> 591	    if (!error) {
> 592	      const spendAction = body.node_type === "image_to_video"
> 593	        ? "canvas_video_spend"
> 594	        : "canvas_image_spend";
> 595	      await admin.rpc("deduct_mco_coins", {
> 596	        p_user_id: user.id,
> 597	        p_amount: creditCost,
> 598	        p_action: spendAction,
> 599	        p_context: {
> 600	          execution_id: execution.id,
> 601	          provider: body.provider,
> 602	          model: body.model,
> 603	          node_type: body.node_type,
> 604	        },
> 605	      });
> 606	
> 607	      // Update project stats
> 608	      await admin
> 609	        .from("vm_canvas_projects")
> 610	        .update({
> 611	          total_executions: (await admin.from("vm_canvas_executions")
> 612	            .select("id", { count: "exact" })
> 613	            .eq("project_id", body.project_id)
> 614	            .eq("status", "success")).count || 0,
> 615	          last_execution_at: new Date().toISOString(),
> 616	        })
> 617	        .eq("id", body.project_id);
> 618	    }
> 619	
> 620	    return new Response(JSON.stringify({
> 621	      execution_id: execution.id,
> 622	      status: finalStatus,
> 623	      output_url: storedUrl || null,
> 624	      asset_id: assetId,
> 625	      credits_charged: error ? 0 : creditCost,
> 626	      credits_remaining: error ? profile.mco_balance : (profile.mco_balance || 0) - creditCost,
> 627	      error: error || undefined,
> 628	    }), { headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } });
> 629	
> 630	  } catch (e) {
> 631	    console.error("canvas-execute error:", e);
> 632	    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Unknown error" }), {
> 633	      status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
> 634	    });
> 635	  }
> 636	});
> 637	
> 638	// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
> 639	// SPACES FASE 1B — LEDGER-FIRST EXECUTION BRANCH
> 640	// SOP: docs/processes/spaces-graph-execution.md (S1-S13) · BoK: docs/bok/spaces-evolution
> 641	// Billing order: 422 guards → begin (row+debit, one tx) → provider (1 retry/5s) →
> 642	// finalize done | error+full refund. Idempotent by node_run_id (duplicate ⇒ zero second debit).
> 643	// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
> 644	
> 645	interface SpacesExecCtx {
> 646	  admin: any;
> 647	  user: { id: string };
> 648	  body: ExecuteRequest;
> 649	  keys: Record<string, string>;
> 650	  creditCost: number;
> 651	  corsHeaders: Record<string, string>;
> 652	  supabaseUrl: string;
> 653	  authHeader: string;
> 654	}
> 655	
> 656	async function sha256Hex(s: string): Promise<string> {
> 657	  const d = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(s));
> 658	  return [...new Uint8Array(d)].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
> 659	}
> 660	
> 661	function spacesJson(status: number, payload: Record<string, unknown>, corsHeaders: Record<string, string>) {
> 662	  return new Response(JSON.stringify(payload), {
> 663	    status, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
> 664	  });
> 665	}
> 666	
> 667	/** finalize_space_generation with ONE retry (security-review MEDIUM 2026-07-02: a transient RPC blip
> 668	 *  must not silently strand the charge). Returns the finalize error (or null) so callers report the
> 669	 *  ledger TRUTH — never claim a refund that didn't commit. */
> 670	async function finalizeWithRetry(
> 671	  admin: any,
> 672	  args: Record<string, unknown>,
> 673	): Promise<{ data: any; error: any }> {
> 674	  let { data, error } = await admin.rpc("finalize_space_generation", args);
> 675	  if (error) {
> 676	    console.error("[canvas-execute:spaces] finalize failed, retrying once:", error);
> 677	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1000));
> 678	    ({ data, error } = await admin.rpc("finalize_space_generation", args));
> 679	    if (error) console.error("[canvas-execute:spaces] finalize retry failed:", error);
> 680	  }
> 681	  return { data, error };
> 682	}
> 683	
> 684	/** Last-resort materialization when finalize(done) fails twice AFTER value was delivered
> 685	 *  (security-review LOW: a delivered-but-stuck 'running' row must not look sweep-refundable —
> 686	 *  the sweep only refunds rows with NO delivered value). Money untouched (refund stays 0). */
> 687	async function materializeDoneFallback(
> 688	  admin: any,
> 689	  nodeRunId: string,
> 690	  patch: Record<string, unknown>,
> 691	) {
> 692	  try {
> 693	    const { error } = await admin
> 694	      .from("generations")
> 695	      .update({ status: "done", ...patch })
> 696	      .eq("node_run_id", nodeRunId)
> 697	      .in("status", ["pending", "running"]);
> 698	    if (error) console.error("[canvas-execute:spaces] done-fallback update failed:", error);
> 699	  } catch (e) {
> 700	    console.error("[canvas-execute:spaces] done-fallback update threw:", e);
> 701	  }
> 702	}
> 703	
> 704	/** SSP-01 OE04 — Daily cap enforcement (dinâmico com base na role). Shared by the legacy and
> 705	 *  Spaces paths. Sums abs(amount) of the canvas_ and spaces ledger actions in the rolling 24h
> 706	 *  window. Fail-open on query error to avoid blocking UX when observability is down.
> 707	 *  Returns the 402 Response when the cap blocks, else null. */
> 708	async function enforceDailyCanvasCap(
> 709	  admin: any,
> 710	  userId: string,
> 711	  creditCost: number,
> 712	  corsHeaders: Record<string, string>,
> 713	): Promise<Response | null> {
> 714	  let dailyCap = 100;
> 715	  const { data: roleRow, error: roleErr } = await admin
> 716	    .from("user_roles")
> 717	    .select("role")
> 718	    .eq("user_id", userId)
> 719	    .maybeSingle();
> 720	
> 721	  if (roleErr) {
> 722	    console.error("[canvas-execute] failed to fetch user role for daily cap:", roleErr);
> 723	  } else if (roleRow?.role === "admin") {
> 724	    dailyCap = 10000;
> 725	  } else if (roleRow?.role === "editor") {
> 726	    dailyCap = 1000;
> 727	  }
> 728	
> 729	  // Explicit action list (was LIKE 'canvas_%spend'): 'spaces.node.run' would silently bypass the
> 730	  // cap under the LIKE pattern (SOP spaces-graph-execution S5). Refund rows use different actions
> 731	  // and are intentionally NOT netted — the cap counts gross spend (conservative).
> 732	  const dayAgoIso = new Date(Date.now() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
> 733	  const { data: spendRows, error: spendErr } = await admin
> 734	    .from("mcoin_transactions")
> 735	    .select("amount")
> 736	    .eq("user_id", userId)
> 737	    .in("action", [
> 738	      "canvas_magic_prompt_spend",
> 739	      "canvas_image_spend",
> 740	      "canvas_video_spend",
> 741	      "spaces.node.run",
> 742	    ])
> 743	    .gte("created_at", dayAgoIso);
> 744	
> 745	  if (spendErr) {
> 746	    console.error("[canvas-execute] daily cap query error:", spendErr);
> 747	  }
> 748	  const spentToday = (spendRows ?? []).reduce(
> 749	    (sum: number, r: { amount: unknown }) => sum + Math.abs(Number(r.amount) || 0),
> 750	    0,
> 751	  );
> 752	  if (spentToday + creditCost > dailyCap) {
> 753	    return new Response(JSON.stringify({
> 754	      error: "Daily Canvas cap reached",
> 755	      cap: dailyCap,
> 756	      spent_today: spentToday,
> 757	      required: creditCost,
> 758	      resets_at: new Date(Date.now() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
> 759	    }), { status: 402, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } });
> 760	  }
> 761	  return null;
> 762	}
> 763	
> 764	/** Pure Higgsfield submit (shared by the legacy vm_canvas_executions path and the Spaces async
> 765	 *  video path — Amendment 2a). No table writes; the caller owns row/ledger side-effects. */
> 766	async function higgsfieldSubmit(o: {
> 767	  apiKey: string;
> 768	  apiSecret: string;
> 769	  body: ExecuteRequest;
> 770	  webhookUrl: string;
> 771	}): Promise<{ ok: true; operationId: string; status?: string } | { ok: false; message: string }> {
> 772	  const duration = typeof o.body.parameters?.duration === "number" ? o.body.parameters.duration : 5;
> 773	  const motionStrength = typeof o.body.parameters?.motion_strength === "number"
> 774	    ? o.body.parameters.motion_strength
> 775	    : 0.5;
> 776	
> 777	  // Routing based on model parameter (aligned with .claude/context/higgsfield-api-validation-2026-05-15.md)
> 778	  const BASE_URL = "https://platform.higgsfield.ai";
> 779	  let modelPath = "higgsfield-ai/dop/standard";
> 780	  if (o.body.model === "dop-lite") {
> 781	    modelPath = "higgsfield-ai/dop/lite";
> 782	  } else if (o.body.model === "dop-turbo") {
> 783	    modelPath = "higgsfield-ai/dop/turbo";
> 784	  } else if (o.body.model === "kling-2.1-pro") {
> 785	    modelPath = "kling-video/v2.1/pro/image-to-video";
> 786	  } else if (o.body.model === "seedance-v1-pro") {
> 787	    modelPath = "bytedance/seedance/v1/pro/image-to-video";
> 788	  }
> 789	
> 790	  const submitUrl = new URL(`${BASE_URL}/${modelPath}`);
> 791	  submitUrl.searchParams.set("hf_webhook", o.webhookUrl);
> 792	
> 793	  let submitResp: Response;
> 794	  try {
> 795	    submitResp = await fetch(submitUrl.toString(), {
> 796	      method: "POST",
> 797	      headers: {
> 798	        Authorization: `Key ${o.apiKey}:${o.apiSecret}`,
> 799	        "Content-Type": "application/json",
> 800	        Accept: "application/json",
> 801	      },
> 802	      body: JSON.stringify({
> 803	        image_url: o.body.input_asset_url,
> 804	        prompt: o.body.prompt,
> 805	        duration,
> 806	        motion_strength: motionStrength,
> 807	      }),
> 808	    });
> 809	  } catch (e) {
> 810	    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Higgsfield submit network error";
> 811	    return { ok: false, message: `Higgsfield submit network error: ${msg}` };
> 812	  }
> 813	
> 814	  if (!submitResp.ok) {
> 815	    const text = await submitResp.text().catch(() => "");
> 816	    let msg = `Higgsfield submit ${submitResp.status}: ${text.slice(0, 200)}`;
> 817	    if (submitResp.status === 401 || submitResp.status === 403) {
> 818	      msg = "Higgsfield: credenciais inválidas";
> 819	    } else if (submitResp.status === 402) {
> 820	      msg = "Higgsfield: saldo de créditos esgotado";
> 821	    } else if (submitResp.status === 429) {
> 822	      msg = "Higgsfield: limite de requisições atingido";
> 823	    }
> 824	    return { ok: false, message: msg };
> 825	  }
> 826	
> 827	  let submitData: { request_id?: string; id?: string; status?: string };
> 828	  try {
> 829	    submitData = await submitResp.json();
> 830	  } catch {
> 831	    return { ok: false, message: "Higgsfield: resposta inválida (JSON parse failed)" };
> 832	  }
> 833	
> 834	  const operationId = submitData.request_id ?? submitData.id;
> 835	  if (!operationId) {
> 836	    return { ok: false, message: "Higgsfield: resposta de submit sem request_id" };
> 837	  }
> 838	  return { ok: true, operationId, status: submitData.status };
> 839	}
> 840	
> 841	/** Amendment 2c (S26): sync voice after the ledger-first debit already committed.
> 842	 *  Reuses tts-speak WITH THE USER'S JWT (it re-validates the session, resolves the per-user
> 843	 *  google_api_key BYOK and returns raw WAV — and it never bills, so there is no double-charge
> 844	 *  hazard). Any synthesis failure after 1 retry refunds in full on the spot. */
> 845	async function executeSpacesVoice(o: {
> 846	  admin: any;
> 847	  body: ExecuteRequest;
> 848	  corsHeaders: Record<string, string>;
> 849	  creditCost: number;
> 850	  generationId: string;
> 851	  supabaseUrl: string;
> 852	  authHeader: string;
> 853	}): Promise<Response> {
> 854	  const { admin, body, corsHeaders, creditCost, generationId, supabaseUrl, authHeader } = o;
> 855	  const t0 = Date.now();
> 856	
> 857	  const refundNow = async (why: string, status: number, errorCode: string) => {
> 858	    console.error(`[canvas-execute:spaces] voice ${errorCode}:`, why);
> 859	    await admin
> 860	      .from("infra_health_logs")
> 861	      .insert({ service: "canvas-execute", status: "unhealthy", event: "spaces_voice_failure" })
> 862	      .then(() => {}, (e: unknown) => console.error("[canvas-execute] health log failed:", e));
> 863	    const { error: finErr } = await finalizeWithRetry(admin, {
> 864	      p_node_run_id: body.node_run_id,
> 865	      p_status: "error",
> 866	      p_refund: creditCost,
> 867	      p_result: { error: why },
> 868	    });
> 869	    return spacesJson(status, {
> 870	      error: errorCode,
> 871	      message: why,
> 872	      execution_id: generationId,
> 873	      ...(finErr ? { refund_pending: true } : { refunded: creditCost }),
> 874	    }, corsHeaders);

> 1466	/** PROC-SPACES-008 — first `done` generation of a Space seeds a Knowledge Mesh observation node.
> 1467	 *  Fail-open: NEVER blocks or fails the run (FMEA-SPACES-010). */
> 1468	async function spacesFirstRunObservation(admin: any, userId: string, spaceId: string) {
> 1469	  try {
> 1470	    const { count } = await admin
> 1471	      .from("generations")
> 1472	      .select("id", { count: "exact", head: true })
> 1473	      .eq("space_id", spaceId)
> 1474	      .eq("status", "done");
> 1475	    if ((count ?? 0) !== 1) return;
> 1476	    await admin.from("mcorch_nodes").insert({
> 1477	      user_id: userId,
> 1478	      node_type: "observation",
> 1479	      name: `spaces-first-run-${spaceId}`,
> 1480	      content: `Primeira geração concluída no Space ${spaceId} (Spaces Fase 1b — canvas→conteúdo pago vivo).`,
> 1481	      metadata: { provider: "spaces", space_id: spaceId, event: "spaces_first_run" },
> 1482	      revenue_impact: 0,
> 1483	      stability_score: 0.85,
> 1484	    });
> 1485	  } catch (e) {
> 1486	    console.error("[canvas-execute:spaces] first-run observation skipped (fail-open):", e);
> 1487	  }
> 1488	}

> 1494	async function generateOpenAI(apiKey: string, body: ExecuteRequest) {
> 1495	  if (!apiKey) throw new Error("OpenAI API key not configured");
> 1496	
> 1497	  const model = body.model || "dall-e-3";
> 1498	  const size = body.parameters?.size || "1024x1024";
> 1499	
> 1500	  // Round 3 hotfix (2026-05-17): OpenAI rejected `response_format` for both
> 1501	  // `gpt-image-1` AND `dall-e-3` on the /v1/images/generations endpoint with
> 1502	  // HTTP 400 "Unknown parameter". We now omit the param — OpenAI defaults to
> 1503	  // returning a URL (for dall-e-*) or b64_json (for gpt-image-1). The caller
> 1504	  // upload block already handles both shapes via {url, b64} return contract.
> 1505	  const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
> 1506	    model,
> 1507	    prompt: body.prompt,
> 1508	    n: 1,
> 1509	    size,
> 1510	  };
> 1511	  // quality + style only apply to dall-e-3; gpt-image-1 ignores them.
> 1512	  if (body.parameters?.quality) payload.quality = body.parameters.quality;
> 1513	  if (body.parameters?.style) payload.style = body.parameters.style;
> 1514	
> 1515	  const resp = await fetch("https://api.openai.com/v1/images/generations", {
> 1516	    method: "POST",
> 1517	    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
> 1518	    body: JSON.stringify(payload),
> 1519	  });
> 1520	
> 1521	  if (!resp.ok) {
> 1522	    const t = await resp.text();
> 1523	    throw new Error(`OpenAI error ${resp.status}: ${t}`);
> 1524	  }
> 1525	
> 1526	  const data = await resp.json();
> 1527	  // gpt-image-1 returns b64_json by default; dall-e-* returns url. Caller's
> 1528	  // upload block prefers b64 (direct upload) but falls back to URL download.
> 1529	  return { url: data.data?.[0]?.url || "", b64: data.data?.[0]?.b64_json || "" };
> 1530	}
> 1531	
> 1532	async function generateReplicate(apiKey: string, body: ExecuteRequest) {
> 1533	  if (!apiKey) throw new Error("Replicate API key not configured");
> 1534	
> 1535	  const modelMap: Record<string, string> = {
> 1536	    "flux-1.1-pro": "black-forest-labs/flux-1.1-pro",
> 1537	    "flux-schnell": "black-forest-labs/flux-schnell",
> 1538	    "sdxl": "stability-ai/sdxl:39ed52f2a78e934b3ba6e2a89f5b1c712de7dfea535525255b1aa35c5565e08b",
> 1539	  };
> 1540	
> 1541	  const modelId = modelMap[body.model] || body.model;
> 1542	  const input: Record<string, any> = {
> 1543	    prompt: body.prompt,
> 1544	    ...(body.negative_prompt ? { negative_prompt: body.negative_prompt } : {}),
> 1545	    ...(body.parameters?.width ? { width: body.parameters.width } : {}),
> 1546	    ...(body.parameters?.height ? { height: body.parameters.height } : {}),
> 1547	    ...(body.parameters?.num_inference_steps ? { num_inference_steps: body.parameters.num_inference_steps } : {}),
> 1548	    ...(body.parameters?.guidance_scale ? { guidance_scale: body.parameters.guidance_scale } : {}),
> 1549	  };
> 1550	
> 1551	  // Cross-node consistency: seed (reproducibility, all models) + reference image
> 1552	  // mapped to each model's img2img param. flux-schnell has no image input → skip.
> 1553	  // SOP: docs/processes/canvas-node-consistency.md. Fail-open if no reference/seed.
> 1554	  const seedVal = typeof body.seed === "number"
> 1555	    ? body.seed
> 1556	    : (typeof body.parameters?.seed === "number" ? body.parameters.seed : undefined);
> 1557	  if (typeof seedVal === "number") input.seed = seedVal;
> 1558	  const refUrl = (body.reference_image_urls ?? []).filter(Boolean)[0];
> 1559	  if (refUrl) {
> 1560	    if (body.model === "flux-1.1-pro") {
> 1561	      input.image_prompt = refUrl; // FLUX 1.1 Pro: composition reference
> 1562	    } else if (body.model === "sdxl") {
> 1563	      input.image = refUrl; // SDXL img2img
> 1564	      input.prompt_strength = typeof body.parameters?.prompt_strength === "number"
> 1565	        ? body.parameters.prompt_strength
> 1566	        : 0.75;
> 1567	    }
> 1568	    // flux-schnell intentionally omitted (text-to-image only).
> 1569	  }
> 1570	
> 1571	  // Create prediction
> 1572	  const createResp = await fetch("https://api.replicate.com/v1/predictions", {
> 1573	    method: "POST",
> 1574	    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
> 1575	    body: JSON.stringify({ model: modelId.includes(":") ? undefined : modelId, version: modelId.includes(":") ? modelId.split(":")[1] : undefined, input }),
> 1576	  });
> 1577	
> 1578	  if (!createResp.ok) {
> 1579	    const t = await createResp.text();
> 1580	    throw new Error(`Replicate error ${createResp.status}: ${t}`);
> 1581	  }
> 1582	
> 1583	  let prediction = await createResp.json();
> 1584	
> 1585	  // Poll for completion (max 120s)
> 1586	  const maxWait = 120_000;
> 1587	  const start = Date.now();
> 1588	  while (prediction.status !== "succeeded" && prediction.status !== "failed" && Date.now() - start < maxWait) {
> 1589	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2000));
> 1590	    const pollResp = await fetch(prediction.urls.get, {
> 1591	      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}` },
> 1592	    });
> 1593	    prediction = await pollResp.json();
> 1594	  }
> 1595	
> 1596	  if (prediction.status === "failed") {
> 1597	    throw new Error(`Replicate failed: ${prediction.error}`);
> 1598	  }
> 1599	
> 1600	  const output = Array.isArray(prediction.output) ? prediction.output[0] : prediction.output;
> 1601	  return { url: output || "" };
> 1602	}
> 1603	
> 1604	async function generateOpenRouter(apiKey: string, body: ExecuteRequest) {
> 1605	  if (!apiKey) throw new Error("OpenRouter API key not configured");
> 1606	
> 1607	  if (isImagePromptTooLong(body.prompt)) {
> 1608	    throw new Error(
> 1609	      `Prompt muito longo (${body.prompt.length} chars; máximo ${MAX_IMAGE_PROMPT_CHARS}). Modelos de imagem interpretam prompts longos como instruções de texto e devolvem texto, não imagem. Use o botão Magic Prompt para reformular como descrição visual concisa.`,
> 1610	    );
> 1611	  }
> 1612	
> 1613	  if (detectInstructionStylePrompt(body.prompt)) {
> 1614	    throw new Error(
> 1615	      `Prompt parece uma instrução de sistema (contém "You are X" / "Output ONLY" / similar), não uma descrição visual. Modelos como Nano Banana 2 responderão com texto em vez de imagem. Reformule como descrição: "A [sujeito], [detalhes], [iluminação/estilo]" ou use o Magic Prompt.`,
> 1616	    );
> 1617	  }
> 1618	
> 1619	  // Round 3 hotfix (2026-05-17): /v1/images/generations returns 404 HTML on
> 1620	  // OpenRouter — endpoint was deprecated. OpenRouter image generation now
> 1621	  // routes through /v1/chat/completions with `modalities: ["image", "text"]`
> 1622	  // for models that support image OUTPUT (e.g. google/gemini-2.5-flash-image-preview,
> 1623	  // black-forest-labs/flux-1.1-pro). Response shape: choices[0].message.content
> 1624	  // is an array of multimodal parts; image parts have type `image_url` with
> 1625	  // `image_url.url` carrying the asset URL.
> 1626	  const model = body.model || "google/gemini-2.5-flash-image";
> 1627	
> 1628	  // Cross-node consistency: attach upstream reference images as image_url parts.
> 1629	  // Gemini ("Nano Banana") accepts up to 4 character refs to preserve identity.
> 1630	  // image_url.url takes both https (preferred — bucket URLs) and data: URIs.
> 1631	  // Fail-open: no references → plain text content (current behavior, zero regression).
> 1632	  const refUrls = (body.reference_image_urls ?? []).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 4);
> 1633	  const userContent: unknown = refUrls.length === 0
> 1634	    ? body.prompt
> 1635	    : [
> 1636	        { type: "text", text: body.prompt },
> 1637	        ...refUrls.map((url) => ({ type: "image_url", image_url: { url } })),
> 1638	      ];
> 1639	
> 1640	  const resp = await fetch("https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions", {
> 1641	    method: "POST",
> 1642	    headers: {
> 1643	      Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`,
> 1644	      "Content-Type": "application/json",
> 1645	      "HTTP-Referer": "https://mcorch.com",
> 1646	      "X-Title": "GCRUX Canvas Studio",
> 1647	    },
> 1648	    body: JSON.stringify({
> 1649	      model,
> 1650	      modalities: ["image", "text"],
> 1651	      messages: [{ role: "user", content: userContent }],
> 1652	    }),
> 1653	  });
> 1654	
> 1655	  if (!resp.ok) {
> 1656	    const t = await resp.text();
> 1657	    throw new Error(`OpenRouter error ${resp.status}: ${t.slice(0, 200)}`);
> 1658	  }
> 1659	
> 1660	  const data = await resp.json();
> 1661	  const message = data?.choices?.[0]?.message;
> 1662	  if (!message) throw new Error("OpenRouter: resposta sem message");
> 1663	
> 1664	  // Two output shapes seen in the wild:
> 1665	  //   1. message.content = string (text only — image missing)
> 1666	  //   2. message.content = [{type:'text',text:...}, {type:'image_url', image_url:{url:'data:...' | 'https://...'}}]
> 1667	  //   3. message.images = [{url:'...'}] (some OR providers return separate field)
> 1668	  let outUrl = "";
> 1669	  let outB64 = "";
> 1670	
> 1671	  if (Array.isArray(message.content)) {
> 1672	    for (const part of message.content) {
> 1673	      if (part?.type === "image_url" && part?.image_url?.url) {
> 1674	        const u = String(part.image_url.url);
> 1675	        if (u.startsWith("data:image/")) {
> 1676	          outB64 = u.split(",", 2)[1] ?? "";
> 1677	        } else {
> 1678	          outUrl = u;
> 1679	        }
> 1680	        break;
> 1681	      }
> 1682	    }
> 1683	  }
> 1684	
> 1685	  if (!outUrl && !outB64 && Array.isArray(message?.images) && message.images.length > 0) {
> 1686	    const first = message.images[0];
> 1687	    let u = "";
> 1688	    if (typeof first === "string") {
> 1689	      u = first;
> 1690	    } else if (first) {
> 1691	      if (first.url) {
> 1692	        u = String(first.url);
> 1693	      } else if (first.image_url?.url) {
> 1694	        u = String(first.image_url.url);
> 1695	      }
> 1696	    }
> 1697	
> 1698	    if (u) {
> 1699	      if (u.startsWith("data:image/")) {
> 1700	        outB64 = u.split(",", 2)[1] ?? "";
> 1701	      } else {
> 1702	        outUrl = u;
> 1703	      }
> 1704	    }
> 1705	  }
> 1706	
> 1707	  if (!outUrl && !outB64) {
> 1708	    // Text-only response = model treated the prompt as a text-generation
> 1709	    // request (typical when prompt looks like an instruction). Pre-flight
> 1710	    // guards catch the obvious cases; this is the runtime safety net.
> 1711	    if (typeof message.content === "string" && message.content.trim().length > 0) {
> 1712	      throw new Error(
> 1713	        `O modelo respondeu com texto em vez de imagem (provável: prompt parece instrução). Reformule como descrição visual ou use o Magic Prompt. Prévia: ${message.content.slice(0, 150)}…`,
> 1714	      );
> 1715	    }
> 1716	    throw new Error(`OpenRouter: resposta multimodal sem image_url. Response keys: ${Object.keys(data || {})}. Message keys: ${Object.keys(message || {})}. Raw response: ${JSON.stringify(data).slice(0, 400)}`);
> 1717	  }
> 1718	
> 1719	  return { url: outUrl, b64: outB64 };
> 1720	}
> 1721	
> 1722	async function generateHiggsfield(apiKey: string, apiSecret: string, body: ExecuteRequest) {
> 1723	  if (!apiKey || !apiSecret) {
> 1724	    throw new Error("Higgsfield credentials not configured (HIGGSFIELD_API_KEY / HIGGSFIELD_API_KEY_SECRET)");
> 1725	  }
> 1726

> 73	COMMENT ON TABLE public.generations IS
> 74	  'spaces-evolution Fase 1b: execution ledger (1 row per node run — FR-SPACES-012). Ledger-first: row+debit in '
> 75	  'one tx via begin_space_generation; refund via finalize_space_generation (claim status IN pending/running, '
> 76	  'first finalizer wins). SELECT-own RLS; service-role writes only. SOP docs/processes/spaces-graph-execution.md.';
> 77	
> 78	-- ── begin_space_generation — atomic ledger-first debit (S1/S2) ──────────────────────────────
> 79	-- INSERT the 'running' row keyed on node_run_id; conflict ⇒ idempotent no-op (returns prior state, ZERO second
> 80	-- debit). Then debits in the SAME tx (deduct RAISE ⇒ full rollback — no orphan row, no charge-without-row).
> 81	-- p_amount 0 (future no-op runs) skips the deduct. Caller (canvas-execute Spaces branch) pre-checks 402s;
> 82	-- the RPC is the race-safe authority. service_role-only via grants (drift guard: audit-privileged-rpc-grants.sh).
> 83	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.begin_space_generation(
> 84	  p_owner_id    uuid,
> 85	  p_space_id    uuid,
> 86	  p_node_id     text,
> 87	  p_node_run_id uuid,
> 88	  p_model_key   text,
> 89	  p_output_type text,
> 90	  p_amount      numeric,
> 91	  p_context     jsonb DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb
> 92	)
> 93	RETURNS jsonb
> 94	LANGUAGE plpgsql
> 95	SECURITY DEFINER
> 96	SET search_path = ''
> 97	AS $$
> 98	DECLARE
> 99	  v_id     uuid;
> 100	  v_dup_id uuid;
> 101	  v_dup_st text;
> 102	BEGIN
> 103	  IF p_amount IS NULL OR p_amount < 0 THEN
> 104	    RAISE EXCEPTION 'begin_space_generation: p_amount must be >= 0' USING errcode = '22023';
> 105	  END IF;
> 106	
> 107	  INSERT INTO public.generations (owner_id, space_id, node_id, node_run_id, output_type, model_key, mco_charged, status)
> 108	  VALUES (p_owner_id, p_space_id, p_node_id, p_node_run_id, p_output_type, p_model_key, p_amount, 'running')
> 109	  ON CONFLICT (node_run_id) DO NOTHING
> 110	  RETURNING id INTO v_id;
> 111	
> 112	  IF v_id IS NULL THEN
> 113	    -- Duplicate node_run_id ⇒ a prior attempt already begun/charged. Return its state; NEVER debit again (S2/G2).
> 114	    -- Owner-filtered (security-review INFO 2026-07-02): a colliding id owned by ANOTHER tenant must not
> 115	    -- disclose that row's existence/state — treat it as a hard conflict instead.
> 116	    SELECT id, status INTO v_dup_id, v_dup_st FROM public.generations
> 117	     WHERE node_run_id = p_node_run_id AND owner_id = p_owner_id;
> 118	    IF v_dup_id IS NULL THEN
> 119	      RAISE EXCEPTION 'begin_space_generation: node_run_id conflict' USING errcode = '23505';
> 120	    END IF;
> 121	    RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'duplicate', true, 'generation_id', v_dup_id, 'status', v_dup_st);
> 122	  END IF;
> 123	
> 124	  IF p_amount > 0 THEN
> 125	    -- Same-tx debit: RAISE (insufficient balance / frozen account) rolls the INSERT back.
> 126	    PERFORM public.deduct_mco_coins(
> 127	      p_owner_id, p_amount, 'spaces.node.run',
> 128	      p_context || jsonb_build_object('node_run_id', p_node_run_id, 'space_id', p_space_id, 'generation_id', v_id)
> 129	    );
> 130	  END IF;
> 131	
> 132	  RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'duplicate', false, 'generation_id', v_id);
> 133	END;
> 134	$$;
> 135	
> 136	-- ── finalize_space_generation — claimed terminal transition + idempotent refund (S6) ────────
> 137	-- Guard `status IN ('pending','running')` ⇒ exactly the FIRST finalizer wins (edge fn OR the stuck-run sweep);
> 138	-- a second call is a no-op (no double refund). Refund clamped 0 ≤ refund ≤ mco_charged (CHECK is the backstop).
> 139	-- uid ALWAYS from the claimed row (tenant safety). Refund credit (add_mco_coins) + symmetric mcoin_transactions
> 140	-- row (action 'spaces.node.refund' — reconciliation key KPI-SPACES-004) commit in the SAME transaction.
> 141	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.finalize_space_generation(
> 142	  p_node_run_id uuid,
> 143	  p_status      text,
> 144	  p_refund      numeric DEFAULT 0,
> 145	  p_asset_id    uuid    DEFAULT NULL,
> 146	  p_result      jsonb   DEFAULT NULL,
> 147	  p_usd_cost    numeric DEFAULT NULL,
> 148	  p_latency_ms  integer DEFAULT NULL
> 149	)
> 150	RETURNS jsonb
> 151	LANGUAGE plpgsql
> 152	SECURITY DEFINER
> 153	SET search_path = ''
> 154	AS $$
> 155	DECLARE
> 156	  v_uid    uuid;
> 157	  v_refund numeric;
> 158	BEGIN
> 159	  IF p_status NOT IN ('done','error') THEN
> 160	    RAISE EXCEPTION 'finalize_space_generation: terminal status required, got %', p_status USING errcode = '22023';
> 161	  END IF;
> 162	
> 163	  UPDATE public.generations
> 164	     SET status       = p_status,
> 165	         asset_id     = COALESCE(p_asset_id, asset_id),
> 166	         result       = COALESCE(p_result, result),
> 167	         usd_cost     = COALESCE(p_usd_cost, usd_cost),
> 168	         latency_ms   = COALESCE(p_latency_ms, latency_ms),
> 169	         mco_refunded = LEAST(GREATEST(COALESCE(p_refund, 0), 0), mco_charged)
> 170	   WHERE node_run_id = p_node_run_id
> 171	     AND status IN ('pending','running')
> 172	   RETURNING owner_id, mco_refunded INTO v_uid, v_refund;
> 173	
> 174	  IF v_uid IS NULL THEN
> 175	    RETURN jsonb_build_object('finalized', false);   -- already terminal → no-op (no double refund)
> 176	  END IF;
> 177	
> 178	  IF v_refund > 0 THEN
> 179	    -- Positive credit (NEVER a negative deduct — anti-mint 20260603220000 lesson). ::integer matches
> 180	    -- add_mco_coins(uuid, integer); all live costs are integers (D6 note).
> 181	    PERFORM public.add_mco_coins(v_uid, v_refund::integer);
> 182	    INSERT INTO public.mcoin_transactions (user_id, action, amount, context)
> 183	    VALUES (v_uid, 'spaces.node.refund', v_refund::integer,
> 184	            jsonb_build_object('node_run_id', p_node_run_id, 'status', p_status));
> 185	    RETURN jsonb_build_object('finalized', true, 'refunded', v_refund);
> 186	  END IF;
> 187	
> 188	  RETURN jsonb_build_object('finalized', true, 'refunded', 0);
> 189	END;
> 190	$$;
> 191	
> 192	-- ── Grants: service-role-only (same-migration trailer — grant-drift lesson 20260701030000) ──
> 193	REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.begin_space_generation(uuid, uuid, text, uuid, text, text, numeric, jsonb)
> 194	  FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
> 195	GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.begin_space_generation(uuid, uuid, text, uuid, text, text, numeric, jsonb)
> 196	  TO service_role;
> 197	
> 198	REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.finalize_space_generation(uuid, text, numeric, uuid, jsonb, numeric, integer)
> 199	  FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
> 200	GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.finalize_space_generation(uuid, text, numeric, uuid, jsonb, numeric, integer)
> 201	  TO service_role;
> 202	
> ```

> 394	  imageUrls?: string[];

> 460	/**
> 461	 * avatar-clone-ai Fatia 2 — voiceClone node (FR-AC-008/009/010/011).
> 462	 * Clones a reference voice (ElevenLabs IVC / Cartesia) then synthesizes a script with it.
> 463	 * Runs against the net-new `generate-voice` Edge Function (NOT canvas-execute).
> 464	 */
> 465	export interface VoiceCloneData {
> 466	  kind: "voiceClone";
> 467	  label: string;
> 468	  /** Provider for both the clone and the synthesis (BYOK per-user). */
> 469	  provider: "elevenlabs" | "cartesia";
> 470	  /** Single language per generation — never code-switch intra-sentence (FM-AC-013). */
> 471	  language: "en" | "pt-BR";
> 472	  /** Uploaded reference-audio sample used to clone the voice. */
> 473	  sampleAudioUrl?: string;
> 474	  sampleStoragePath?: string;
> 475	  /** voice_profiles row id — set after a successful clone; required to synthesize. */
> 476	  voiceProfileId?: string;
> 477	  /** Text to synthesize with the cloned voice. */
> 478	  prompt: string;
> 479	  status: NodeStatus;
> 480	  output?: {
> 481	    audioUrl?: string;
> 482	    creativeAssetId?: string;
> 483	    generatedAt: string;
> 484	  };
> 485	  error?: string;
> 486	}
> 487	
> 488	/**
> 489	 * avatar-clone-ai Fatia 3b — avatarIdentity node (FR-AC-012).
> 490	 * Holds a consented reference-portrait set + the trained identity profile. Trains against the
> 491	 * net-new `avatar-identity-train` Edge Function (gated OTD-AC-017) — NOT canvas-execute. The
> 492	 * Identity Gate runs the VLM-soft compare (FR-AC-013b, src/lib/avatar-identity.ts) post-generation.
> 493	 */
> 494	export interface AvatarIdentityData {
> 495	  kind: "avatarIdentity";
> 496	  label: string;
> 497	  /** self_clone (titular cloning themselves) drives the FR-AC-032 verification + compliance template. */
> 498	  personaType: "self_clone" | "fictional";
> 499	  /** Identity ladder tier (1 InstantID/IP-Adapter · 2 PuLID-Flux · 3 LoRA per-person). */
> 500	  tier: 1 | 2 | 3;
> 501	  provider: "higgsfield" | "lora_flux";
> 502	  /** Identity Gate mode — VLM-soft MVP default (FR-AC-013/014); arcface deferred OTD-AC-015. */
> 503	  gateMode: "vlm_soft" | "arcface";
> 504	  /** Uploaded reference-portrait signed URLs (≥20 recommended for Soul ID). */
> 505	  referenceUrls: string[];
> 506	  referenceStoragePaths?: string[];
> 507	  /** avatar_identities row id — set after a successful train (gated OTD-AC-017). */
> 508	  avatarIdentityId?: string;
> 509	  status: NodeStatus;
> 510	  output?: {
> 511	    /** last generated frame URL that ran through the Identity Gate */
> 512	    frameUrl?: string;
> 513	    softMatch?: "pass" | "review" | "fail";
> 514	    generatedAt: string;
> 515	  };
> 516	  error?: string;
> 517	}
> 518	
> 519	export type CanvasNodeData =
> 520	  | GenerateImageData
> 521	  | CharacterReferenceData
> 522	  | SceneComposeData
> 523	  | StyleTransferData
> 524	  | ImageToVideoData
> 525	  | VoiceCloneData
> 526	  | AvatarIdentityData;

> 528	export type CanvasNode = Node<CanvasNodeData, CanvasNodeKind>;
> 529	export type CanvasEdge = Edge;
> 530	
> 531	export interface CanvasGraph {
> 532	  nodes: CanvasNode[];
> 533	  edges: CanvasEdge[];
> 534	}
> 535	
> 536	export const EMPTY_GRAPH: CanvasGraph = { nodes: [], edges: [] };

> 553	export function laneIndexFromY(y: number): number {
> 554	  return Math.max(0, Math.floor(y / LANE_HEIGHT));

> 751	/* ── Canvas-execute payload helper ───────────────────────────────────── */
> 752	
> 753	/**
> 754	 * Helper to compile prompt incorporating project level StyleSettings
> 755	 */
> 756	export function compileFinalPrompt(
> 757	  prompt: string,
> 758	  styleSettings?: StyleSettings,
> 759	  inheritStyle?: boolean,
> 760	): string {
> 761	  if (!inheritStyle || !styleSettings) return prompt;
> 762	
> 763	  const parts: string[] = [];
> 764	  if (prompt.trim()) {
> 765	    parts.push(prompt.trim());
> 766	  }
> 767	
> 768	  const { stylePreset, mood, palette } = styleSettings;
> 769	
> 770	  if (mood && mood.trim()) {
> 771	    parts.push(`mood: ${mood.trim()}`);
> 772	  }
> 773	
> 774	  if (stylePreset && stylePreset !== "none") {
> 775	    const presetSuffixes: Record<StylePresetId, string> = {
> 776	      none: "",
> 777	      cinematic: "cinematic film still, anamorphic lens, dramatic lighting, 35mm film grain, shallow depth of field",
> 778	      photorealistic: "highly detailed photorealistic image, 8k resolution, natural lighting, high dynamic range",
> 779	      anime: "anime illustration style, cel shaded, vibrant colors, expressive line art",
> 780	      illustration: "artistic illustration, hand-drawn texture, stylized composition",
> 781	      noir: "film noir aesthetic, high contrast black and white, dramatic shadows, moody lighting",
> 782	      documentary: "documentary style photography, candid capture, natural lighting, realistic grain",
> 783	    };
> 784	    const suffix = presetSuffixes[stylePreset];
> 785	    if (suffix) {
> 786	      parts.push(suffix);
> 787	    }
> 788	  }
> 789	
> 790	  if (palette && palette.length > 0) {
> 791	    const validColors = palette.filter(c => c && c.trim());
> 792	    if (validColors.length > 0) {
> 793	      parts.push(`color palette: ${validColors.join(", ")}`);
> 794	    }
> 795	  }
> 796	
> 797	  return parts.join(", ");
> 798	}
> 799	
> 800	/**
> 801	 * Translate a typed node's data into the request body accepted by the
> 802	 * `canvas-execute` Edge Function. Soul models split into provider/model
> 803	 * plus parameters.resolution/batch so the function's tiering helper
> 804	 * (`resolveHiggsfieldSoulKey`) computes the correct cost.
> 805	 */
> 806	export function resolveExecutePayload(
> 807	  data: CanvasNodeData,
> 808	  styleSettings?: StyleSettings,
> 809	): {
> 810	  node_type: string;
> 811	  provider: ProviderId;
> 812	  model: string;
> 813	  prompt: string;
> 814	  negative_prompt?: string;
> 815	  parameters: Record<string, unknown>;
> 816	} | null {
> 817	  switch (data.kind) {
> 818	    case "generateImage": {
> 819	      const info = getImageModelInfo(data.modelId);
> 820	      const parameters: Record<string, unknown> = {
> 821	        aspect_ratio: data.aspectRatio,
> 822	        controls: data.controls,
> 823	        seed: data.fixedSeed && data.seed != null ? data.seed : undefined,
> 824	      };
> 825	      if (info.resolution) parameters.resolution = info.resolution;
> 826	      if (info.batch) parameters.batch = info.batch;
> 827	      return {
> 828	        node_type: "generate_image",
> 829	        provider: info.provider,
> 830	        model: info.modelParam,
> 831	        prompt: compileFinalPrompt(data.prompt, styleSettings, data.inheritStyle),
> 832	        negative_prompt: data.negativePrompt || undefined,
> 833	        parameters,
> 834	      };
> 835	    }
> 836	    case "sceneCompose": {
> 837	      const info = getImageModelInfo(data.modelId);
> 838	      const parameters: Record<string, unknown> = {
> 839	        aspect_ratio: data.aspectRatio,
> 840	      };
> 841	      if (info.resolution) parameters.resolution = info.resolution;
> 842	      if (info.batch) parameters.batch = info.batch;
> 843	      return {
> 844	        node_type: "scene_compose",
> 845	        provider: info.provider,
> 846	        model: info.modelParam,
> 847	        prompt: compileFinalPrompt(data.prompt, styleSettings, data.inheritStyle),
> 848	        parameters,
> 849	      };
> 850	    }
> 851	    case "styleTransfer": {
> 852	      const preset = STYLE_TRANSFER_PRESETS.find((p) => p.id === data.presetId);
> 853	      return {
> 854	        node_type: "style_transfer",
> 855	        provider: "higgsfield",
> 856	        model: "soul",
> 857	        prompt: preset?.suffix ?? "",
> 858	        parameters: {
> 859	          strength: data.strength,
> 860	          preset_id: data.presetId,
> 861	          resolution: "720p",
> 862	          batch: 1,
> 863	        },
> 864	      };
> 865	    }
> 866	    case "imageToVideo": {
> 867	      const info = getVideoModelInfo(data.videoModel);
> 868	      return {
> 869	        node_type: "image_to_video",
> 870	        provider: info.provider,
> 871	        model: info.modelParam,
> 872	        prompt: data.prompt,
> 873	        parameters: {
> 874	          duration: data.duration,
> 875	          aspect_ratio: data.aspectRatio,
> 876	          motion_strength: data.motionStrength,
> 877	          async_only: info.asyncOnly ?? false,
> 878	        },
> 879	      };
> 880	    }
> 881	    case "characterReference":
> 882	      // CharacterReference doesn't trigger canvas-execute by itself —
> 883	      // it's a data source consumed by downstream sceneCompose/styleTransfer.
> 884	      return null;
> 885	    case "voiceClone":
> 886	      // voiceClone runs against the net-new `generate-voice` Edge Function (FR-AC-009),
> 887	      // NOT canvas-execute — its inspector calls useAvatarVoice directly.
> 888	      return null;
> 889	    case "avatarIdentity":
> 890	      // avatarIdentity trains against the net-new `avatar-identity-train` Edge Function
> 891	      // (FR-AC-012, gated OTD-AC-017), NOT canvas-execute — inspector handles it directly.
> 892	      return null;
> 893	  }
> 894	}
> 895	
> 896	/**
> 897	 * Type-guard helpers (used by node components and inspectors to narrow
> 898	 * the union via switch/case on `kind`).
> 899	 */
> 900	export function isGenerateImage(data: CanvasNodeData): data is GenerateImageData {
> 901	  return data.kind === "generateImage";
> 902	}
> 903	export function isCharacterReference(data: CanvasNodeData): data is CharacterReferenceData {
> 904	  return data.kind === "characterReference";
> 905	}
> 906	export function isSceneCompose(data: CanvasNodeData): data is SceneComposeData {
> 907	  return data.kind === "sceneCompose";
> 908	}
> 909	export function isStyleTransfer(data: CanvasNodeData): data is StyleTransferData {
> 910	  return data.kind === "styleTransfer";
> 911	}
> 912	export function isImageToVideo(data: CanvasNodeData): data is ImageToVideoData {
> 913	  return data.kind === "imageToVideo";
> 914	}
> 915	export function isVoiceClone(data: CanvasNodeData): data is VoiceCloneData {
> 916	  return data.kind === "voiceClone";
> 917	}
> 918	export function isAvatarIdentity(data: CanvasNodeData): data is AvatarIdentityData {
> 919	  return data.kind === "avatarIdentity";
> 920	}
> 921	
> 922	/** Resolve the mcoCoins cost of a single node from MODEL_INFO/VIDEO_MODEL_INFO. */
> 923	export function estimateNodeCost(data: CanvasNodeData): number {
> 924	  switch (data.kind) {
> 925	    case "generateImage":
> 926	      return getImageModelInfo(data.modelId).coinCost;
> 927	    case "sceneCompose":
> 928	      return data.cost;
> 929	    case "styleTransfer":
> 930	      return data.cost;
> 931	    case "imageToVideo":
> 932	      return getVideoModelInfo(data.videoModel).coinCost;
> 933	    case "characterReference":
> 934	      return 0;
> 935	    case "voiceClone":

> 21	export function parseSpaceRow(row: SpaceRow): SpaceEntity {

> 31	    const p = SpaceEdgeGuard.safeParse(e);
> 32	    if (p.success) edges.push(p.data as unknown as SpaceEdge);
> 33	    else console.warn('[spaces] dropped invalid edge on hydrate (FMEA-SPACES-007)', p.error.issues[0]);
> 34	  }

> 36	  return {

> 40	    pages: (Array.isArray(row.pages) ? row.pages : []) as SpacePage[],
> 41	    nodes,

> 43	    viewport: vp.success ? vp.data : DEFAULT_VIEWPORT,
> 44	    isTemplate: row.is_template,
> 45	    createdAt: row.created_at,

> 47	  };
> 48	}
> 49

> 55	export function useSpacesList() {
> 56	  const { user } = useAuth();
> 57	  return useQuery({
> 58	    queryKey: ['spaces'],
> 59	    enabled: !!user,
> 60	    queryFn: async (): Promise<SpaceRow[]> => {
> 61	      const { data, error } = await supabase
> 62	        .from('spaces').select('*').order('updated_at', { ascending: false });
> 63	      if (error) throw error;
> 64	      return (data ?? []) as SpaceRow[];
> 65	    },
> 66	  });
> 67	}
> 68	
> 69	export function useSpace(spaceId?: string) {
> 70	  const { user } = useAuth();
> 71	  return useQuery({
> 72	    queryKey: ['space', spaceId],
> 73	    enabled: !!user && !!spaceId,
> 74	    queryFn: async (): Promise<SpaceEntity | null> => {
> 75	      const { data, error } = await supabase
> 76	        .from('spaces').select('*').eq('id', spaceId!).maybeSingle();
> 77	      if (error) throw error;
> 78	      return data ? parseSpaceRow(data as SpaceRow) : null;   // RLS: outro tenant → null → "não encontrado"
> 79	    },
> 80	  });
> 81	}
> 82	
> 83	export function useCreateSpace() {
> 84	  const qc = useQueryClient();
> 85	  const { user } = useAuth();
> 86	  return useMutation({
> 87	    mutationFn: async (title: string): Promise<string> => {
> 88	      if (!user) throw new Error('Sessão expirada — faça login novamente.');
> 89	      const { data, error } = await supabase
> 90	        .from('spaces')
> 91	        .insert({ owner_id: user.id, title: title.trim() || 'Untitled space' })
> 92	        .select('id').single();
> 93	      if (error) throw error;
> 94	      return data.id as string;
> 95	    },
> 96	    onSuccess: () => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['spaces'] }),
> 97	  });
> 98	}
> 99	
> 100	/** Strip xyflow-volatile fields before persisting (selection/drag state is session-local).
> 101	 *  Transient run statuses (pending/running) reset to idle (SOP spaces-graph-execution S9) —
> 102	 *  a reload must never hydrate a ghost in-flight run; done/error/blocked persist as history. */
> 103	function serializeGraph(s: SpaceGraphSnapshot) {
> 104	  return {
> 105	    nodes: s.nodes.map((n) => ({
> 106	      ...n, selected: undefined, dragging: undefined, measured: undefined,
> 107	      data: (n.data.status === 'pending' || n.data.status === 'running')
> 108	        ? { ...n.data, status: 'idle' as const }
> 109	        : n.data,
> 110	    })),
> 111	    edges: s.edges.map((e) => ({ ...e, selected: undefined })),
> 112	    viewport: s.viewport,
> 113	  };
> 114	}
> 115	
> 116	export function useSaveSpaceGraph() {
> 117	  const qc = useQueryClient();
> 118	  return useMutation({
> 119	    mutationFn: async ({ spaceId, snapshot }: { spaceId: string; snapshot: SpaceGraphSnapshot }) => {
> 120	      // Hydration-race guard (FMEA-SPACES-006, mirrors useSaveCanvasGraph): NEVER overwrite a non-empty
> 121	      // saved graph with an empty one — a debounce flush before hydrate would clobber the DB row.
> 122	      if (snapshot.nodes.length === 0) {
> 123	        const { data } = await supabase.from('spaces').select('graph').eq('id', spaceId).maybeSingle();
> 124	        if (data && spaceNodeCount(data as Pick<SpaceRow, 'graph'>) > 0) {
> 125	          console.warn('[spaces] save skipped: empty snapshot over non-empty graph (FMEA-SPACES-006)');
> 126	          return { skipped: true };
> 127	        }
> 128	      }
> 129	      const { error } = await supabase
> 130	        .from('spaces').update({ graph: serializeGraph(snapshot) }).eq('id', spaceId);
> 131	      if (error) throw error;
> 132	      return { skipped: false };
> 133	    },
> 134	    onSuccess: (_r, vars) => {
> 135	      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['spaces'] });
> 136	      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['space', vars.spaceId] });
> 137	    },
> 138	  });
> 139	}
> 140	
> ```

> 115	export function collectDescendants(nodeId: string, edges: SpaceEdge[]): Set<string> {

> 136	/**

> 140	 */
> 141	export function resolveUpstreamInputs(
> 142	  node: SpaceNode,

> 144	  outputs: Map<string, NodeOutput>,
> 145	): UpstreamInputs {
> 146	  const result: UpstreamInputs = { referenceImages: [] };
> 147	  for (const e of edges) {
> 148	    if (e.target !== node.id) continue;
> 149	    const up = outputs.get(e.source);
> 150	    if (!up) continue;
> 151	    if ((e.targetHandle === 'prompt' || e.targetHandle === 'text') && up.type === 'text') result.promptText = up.value;
> 152	    if (e.targetHandle === 'reference' && up.type === 'image') result.referenceImages.push(up.value);
> 153	    if ((e.targetHandle === 'image-1' || e.targetHandle === 'image-2') && up.type === 'image') {
> 154	      // S31: composer handles — image-1 first (unshift), image-2 appended
> 155	      if (e.targetHandle === 'image-1') result.referenceImages.unshift(up.value);

> 157	    }
> 158	    if (e.targetHandle === 'first-frame' && up.type === 'image') result.firstFrameImage = up.value;
> 159	  }
> 160	  return result;
> 161	}

> 151	export function useSaveCanvasGraph() {
> 152	  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
> 153	
> 154	  return useMutation({
> 155	    mutationFn: async ({ projectId, graph, viewport }: {
> 156	      projectId: string;
> 157	      graph: PersistedCanvasGraph;
> 158	      viewport?: { x: number; y: number; zoom: number };
> 159	    }) => {
> 160	      // Hydration-race guard (data-loss fix): NEVER overwrite a non-empty saved graph with an empty one.
> 161	      // useAutoSave can flush (debounce / pagehide / unmount) BEFORE initialize() hydrates the store, so the
> 162	      // empty boot state {nodes:[]} would clobber a rich DB row → all canvas nodes lost (assets survive in
> 163	      // vm_canvas_executions, which is why the project still shows assets but no nodes). When the incoming
> 164	      // graph is empty, read-before-write and skip the clobber (viewport-only updates still persist).
> 165	      if (graph.nodes.length === 0) {
> 166	        const { data: existing } = await supabase
> 167	          .from("vm_canvas_projects")
> 168	          .select("graph")
> 169	          .eq("id", projectId)
> 170	          .maybeSingle();
> 171	        const existingNodes = (existing?.graph as PersistedCanvasGraph | null)?.nodes ?? [];
> 172	        if (existingNodes.length > 0) {
> 173	          console.warn(
> 174	            `[saveCanvasGraph] blocked empty-graph overwrite of ${existingNodes.length}-node project ${projectId} (hydration race)`,
> 175	          );
> 176	          if (viewport) {
> 177	            await supabase.from("vm_canvas_projects").update({ viewport }).eq("id", projectId);
> 178	          }
> 179	          return;
> 180	        }
> 181	      }
> 182	
> 183	      const updateData: any = { graph };
> 184	      if (viewport) updateData.viewport = viewport;
> 185	
> 186	      const { error } = await supabase
> 187	        .from("vm_canvas_projects")
> 188	        .update(updateData)
> 189	        .eq("id", projectId);
> 190	      if (error) throw error;
> 191	    },
> 192	    onSuccess: (_, vars) => {
> 193	      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["vm-canvas-project", vars.projectId] });
> 194	    },
> 195	  });
> 196	}

> 221	    mutationFn: async (params: {
> 222	      project_id: string;
> 223	      node_id: string;
> 224	      node_type: string;
> 225	      provider: string;
> 226	      model: string;

> 237	      reference_image_urls?: string[];

> 239	      const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke("canvas-execute", { body: params });
> 240	      if (error) {
> 241	        let message = error.message;
> 242	        try {
> 243	          const ctx = (error as { context?: Response }).context;
> 244	          const parsed = ctx ? await ctx.json() : null;
> 245	          if (parsed?.error) message = parsed.error as string;
> 246	        } catch {
> 247	          // Fall back to original error message
> 248	        }
> 249	        throw new Error(message);
> 250	      }
> 251	      return data;
> 252	    },
> 253	    onSuccess: (_, vars) => {
> 254	      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["vm-canvas-project", vars.project_id] });
> 255	      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["vm-canvas-assets"] });
> 256	      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["vm-canvas-executions"] });

> 12	  classifyNode, buildExecutePayload, resolveUpstreamInputs, collectDescendants,
> 13	  projectSpacesGraphCost, rowToGeneration, missingVideoInput, missingComposeInput, nodeTypeId,
> 14	  type NodeOutput, type GenerationRow,
> 15	} from '@/lib/spaces-execution';
> 16	import { useSpaceSession } from '@/hooks/useSpaceSession';
> 17	import { toast } from '@/hooks/use-toast';
> 18	import type { SpaceNode } from '@/lib/spaces-schema';
> 19	
> 20	const PAGE_SIZE = 24;
> 21	
> 22	interface ExecResponse {
> 23	  execution_id: string;
> 24	  status: string;
> 25	  output_url?: string;
> 26	  enhanced_prompt?: string;
> 27	  duplicate?: boolean;
> 28	  credits_charged: number;
> 29	  operation_id?: string; // async video (202/queued — Amendment 2a S14)
> 30	}
> 31	
> 32	/** S19 — async video poller: reads the node's own `generations` row (RLS own) every POLL_MS until
> 33	 *  terminal, then flips the node + invalidates the cluster. Timeout leaves the node `running`
> 34	 *  honestly (the S17 sweep is the money backstop). */
> 35	const POLL_MS = 5_000;
> 36	const POLL_MAX_MS = 12 * 60_000;
> 37	function watchVideoGeneration(
> 38	  nodeId: string,
> 39	  nodeRunId: string,
> 40	  spaceId: string,
> 41	  onSettled: (status: 'done' | 'error', refunded: number) => void,
> 42	) {
> 43	  const startedAt = Date.now();
> 44	  const tick = async () => {
> 45	    try {
> 46	      const { data } = await supabase
> 47	        .from('generations')
> 48	        .select('status, mco_refunded')
> 49	        .eq('node_run_id', nodeRunId)
> 50	        .maybeSingle();
> 51	      const status = data?.status;
> 52	      if (status === 'done' || status === 'error') {
> 53	        onSettled(status, Number(data?.mco_refunded) || 0);
> 54	        return;
> 55	      }
> 56	    } catch { /* transient read error — keep polling */ }
> 57	    if (Date.now() - startedAt < POLL_MAX_MS) {
> 58	      setTimeout(tick, POLL_MS);
> 59	    } else {
> 60	      toast({
> 61	        title: 'Vídeo ainda processando',
> 62	        description: 'O cluster do nó atualiza quando concluir; execuções travadas são estornadas automaticamente.',
> 63	      });
> 64	    }
> 65	  };
> 66	  setTimeout(tick, POLL_MS);
> 67	}
> 68	
> 69	/** Structured run failure — kind drives node status + run control (FRD error table 04:100-107). */
> 70	class RunNodeError extends Error {
> 71	  kind: 'blocked' | 'error' | 'abort';
> 72	  constructor(kind: 'blocked' | 'error' | 'abort', message: string) {
> 73	    super(message);
> 74	    this.kind = kind;
> 75	  }
> 76	}
> 77	
> 78	/** Extract {status, code, message} from a FunctionsHttpError and map to run semantics. */
> 79	async function toRunError(error: unknown): Promise<RunNodeError> {
> 80	  const ctx = (error as { context?: Response })?.context;
> 81	  let status = 0;
> 82	  let code = '';
> 83	  let message = '';
> 84	  try {
> 85	    status = (ctx as Response | undefined)?.status ?? 0;
> 86	    const body = await (ctx as Response | undefined)?.json?.();
> 87	    code = (body?.error as string) ?? '';
> 88	    message = (body?.message as string) ?? code;
> 89	  } catch { /* body was not JSON */ }
> 90	
> 91	  if (status === 402) {
> 92	    const daily = /daily|cap/i.test(code);
> 93	    return new RunNodeError('blocked', daily
> 94	      ? 'Teto diário de mcoCoins do dia já atingido.'
> 95	      : 'Saldo de mcoCoins insuficiente.');
> 96	  }
> 97	  if (status === 404) return new RunNodeError('abort', 'Space não encontrado.');
> 98	  if (status === 422) return new RunNodeError('error', message || 'Parâmetros inválidos para este nó.');
> 99	  if (status === 502) return new RunNodeError('error', message || 'Falha do provedor — o valor debitado foi estornado.');
> 100	  return new RunNodeError('error', message || (error as Error)?.message || 'Falha na execução do nó.');
> 101	}
> 102	
> 103	/** Seed the in-run output map with each node's LATEST done generation (S11 threading across runs). */
> 104	async function seedOutputs(spaceId: string, outputs: Map<string, NodeOutput>) {
> 105	  const { data } = await supabase
> 106	    .from('generations')
> 107	    .select('node_id, output_type, result, created_at')
> 108	    .eq('space_id', spaceId)
> 109	    .eq('status', 'done')
> 110	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
> 111	    .limit(200);
> 112	  for (const row of data ?? []) {
> 113	    if (outputs.has(row.node_id)) continue; // newest wins (ordered DESC)
> 114	    const result = (row.result ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
> 115	    if (row.output_type === 'text' && typeof result.enhanced_prompt === 'string') {
> 116	      outputs.set(row.node_id, { type: 'text', value: result.enhanced_prompt });
> 117	    } else if (row.output_type === 'image' && typeof result.output_url === 'string') {
> 118	      outputs.set(row.node_id, { type: 'image', value: result.output_url });
> 119	    }
> 120	  }
> 121	}
> 122	
> 123	export function useGenerationLedger(spaceId?: string) {
> 124	  const qc = useQueryClient();
> 125	  const { ensureFresh } = useSpaceSession();
> 126	
> 127	  /** Dry-run projection (BR-SPACES-005/008) — pure client math, zero débito, zero server calls. */
> 128	  const estimate = useCallback(
> 129	    () => projectSpacesGraphCost(useSpacesStore.getState().nodes),
> 130	    [],
> 131	  );
> 132	
> 133	  const runGraph = useCallback(async () => {
> 134	    if (!spaceId) return;
> 135	    const store = useSpacesStore.getState();
> 136	    if (store.runState === 'running') return;
> 137	
> 138	    if (!(await ensureFresh())) {
> 139	      toast({ title: 'Sessão expirada — faça login novamente.', variant: 'destructive' });
> 140	      return;
> 141	    }
> 142	
> 143	    let layers: SpaceNode[][];
> 144	    try {
> 145	      layers = getSpaceLayers(store.nodes, store.edges);
> 146	    } catch {
> 147	      toast({ title: 'Ciclo detectado no grafo — remova a conexão circular.', variant: 'destructive' });
> 148	      return;
> 149	    }
> 150	
> 151	    const executables = store.nodes.filter((n) => classifyNode(n) === 'executable');
> 152	    if (executables.length === 0) {
> 153	      toast({ title: 'Nenhum nó executável na Fase 1', description: 'Adicione um Gerador de imagem, Assistant ou Gerador de vídeo.' });
> 154	      return;
> 155	    }
> 156	
> 157	    store.setRunState('running');
> 158	    const outputs = new Map<string, NodeOutput>();
> 159	    try {
> 160	      await seedOutputs(spaceId, outputs);
> 161	    } catch { /* seeding is best-effort — in-run outputs still thread */ }
> 162	
> 163	    const blockedIds = new Set<string>();
> 164	    let hadError = false;
> 165	    let ranAny = false;
> 166	    let aborted = false;
> 167	    let warnedUnsupported = false;
> 168	
> 169	    const runNode = async (node: SpaceNode) => {
> 170	      const st = useSpacesStore.getState();
> 171	      const upstream = resolveUpstreamInputs(node, st.edges, outputs);
> 172	      // S21: video batch is ALWAYS 1 in the 2a slice (accidental ×8 would debit up to 1,280 mco).
> 173	      const isVideo = nodeTypeId(node) === 'video-generator';
> 174	      const batch = isVideo ? 1 : Math.max(1, node.data.batch ?? 1);
> 175	      if (missingVideoInput(node, upstream)) {
> 176	        st.updateNodeStatus(node.id, 'idle');
> 177	        toast({ title: `"${node.data.label}" sem imagem no first-frame — pulado (nada cobrado).`,
> 178	          description: 'Conecte a saída de um Gerador de imagem (done) ao handle first-frame.' });
> 179	        return;
> 180	      }
> 181	      if (missingComposeInput(node, upstream)) {
> 182	        st.updateNodeStatus(node.id, 'idle');
> 183	        toast({ title: `"${node.data.label}" sem imagem — pulado (nada cobrado).`,
> 184	          description: 'Anexe até 2 imagens no Designer ou conecte-as nos handles image-1/image-2.' });
> 185	        return;
> 186	      }
> 187	      st.updateNodeStatus(node.id, 'running');
> 188	      for (let i = 0; i < batch; i++) {
> 189	        // S3: node_run_id minted ONCE per attempt (idempotency anchor server-side).
> 190	        const nodeRunId = crypto.randomUUID();
> 191	        const payload = buildExecutePayload(node, spaceId, nodeRunId, upstream);
> 192	        if (!payload) {
> 193	          st.updateNodeStatus(node.id, 'idle');
> 194	          toast({ title: `"${node.data.label}" sem prompt — pulado (nada cobrado).` });
> 195	          return;
> 196	        }
> 197	        const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke('canvas-execute', { body: payload });
> 198	        if (error) throw await toRunError(error);
> 199	        const res = data as ExecResponse;
> 200	        ranAny = true;
> 201	        if (res.status === 'queued') {
> 202	          // S19: async dispatch — the node stays `running`; the poller settles it from the row truth.
> 203	          watchVideoGeneration(node.id, nodeRunId, spaceId, (status, refunded) => {
> 204	            useSpacesStore.getState().updateNodeStatus(node.id, status);
> 205	            qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['generations', spaceId, node.id] });
> 206	            toast(status === 'done'
> 207	              ? { title: `"${node.data.label}" — vídeo pronto ✓` }
> 208	              : { title: `"${node.data.label}" — geração de vídeo falhou`,
> 209	                  description: refunded > 0 ? `Estorno de ${refunded} mcoCoins aplicado.` : undefined,
> 210	                  variant: 'destructive' });
> 211	          });
> 212	          qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['generations', spaceId, node.id] });
> 213	          return; // node stays running — do NOT mark done here
> 214	        }
> 215	        if (res.enhanced_prompt) outputs.set(node.id, { type: 'text', value: res.enhanced_prompt });
> 216	        else if (res.output_url) outputs.set(node.id, { type: 'image', value: res.output_url });
> 217	      }
> 218	      st.updateNodeStatus(node.id, 'done');
> 219	      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['generations', spaceId, node.id] });
> 220	    };
> 221	
> 222	    for (const layer of layers) {
> 223	      if (aborted) break;
> 224	
> 225	      const unsupported = layer.filter((n) => classifyNode(n) === 'unsupported');
> 226	      if (unsupported.length > 0 && !warnedUnsupported) {
> 227	        warnedUnsupported = true;
> 228	        toast({
> 229	          title: 'Alguns nós não são suportados na Fase 1',
> 230	          description: 'Eles permanecem como estão — nada é cobrado por eles.',
> 231	        });
> 232	      }
> 233	
> 234	      const toRun = layer.filter((n) => classifyNode(n) === 'executable' && !blockedIds.has(n.id));
> 235	      if (toRun.length === 0) continue;
> 236	
> 237	      const results = await Promise.allSettled(toRun.map((n) => runNode(n)));
> 238	      results.forEach((r, i) => {
> 239	        if (r.status !== 'rejected') return;
> 240	        const node = toRun[i];
> 241	        const err = r.reason instanceof RunNodeError ? r.reason : new RunNodeError('error', String(r.reason));
> 242	        hadError = true;
> 243	        const st = useSpacesStore.getState();
> 244	        if (err.kind === 'blocked' || err.kind === 'abort') {
> 245	          st.updateNodeStatus(node.id, 'blocked');
> 246	          aborted = true; // 402/404: no point continuing — remaining nodes stay untouched
> 247	        } else {
> 248	          st.updateNodeStatus(node.id, 'error');
> 249	          for (const descId of collectDescendants(node.id, st.edges)) {
> 250	            blockedIds.add(descId);
> 251	            if (st.nodes.some((n) => n.id === descId && classifyNode(n) === 'executable')) {
> 252	              st.updateNodeStatus(descId, 'blocked');
> 253	            }
> 254	          }
> 255	        }
> 256	        toast({ title: `"${node.data.label}" falhou`, description: err.message, variant: 'destructive' });
> 257	      });
> 258	    }
> 259	
> 260	    useSpacesStore.getState().setRunState(hadError ? 'partial' : ranAny ? 'done' : 'idle');
> 261	  }, [spaceId, ensureFresh, qc]);
> 262	
> 263	  return { estimate, runGraph };
> 264	}
> 265	
> 266	/** Cluster reader (FR-SPACES-012/002): lazy ≤24 cards/page, cursor = created_at DESC, staleTime 60s. */
> 267	export function useGenerationCluster(spaceId?: string, nodeId?: string | null) {
> 268	  return useInfiniteQuery({
> 269	    queryKey: ['generations', spaceId, nodeId],
> 270	    enabled: !!spaceId && !!nodeId,
> 271	    staleTime: 60_000,
> 272	    initialPageParam: null as string | null,
> 273	    queryFn: async ({ pageParam }) => {
> 274	      let q = supabase
> 275	        .from('generations')
> 276	        .select('*')
> 277	        .eq('space_id', spaceId!)
> 278	        .eq('node_id', nodeId!)
> 279	        .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
> 280	        .limit(PAGE_SIZE);
> 281	      if (pageParam) q = q.lt('created_at', pageParam);
> 282	      const { data, error } = await q;
> 283	      if (error) throw error;
> 284	      return ((data ?? []) as GenerationRow[]).map(rowToGeneration);
> 285	    },
> 286	    getNextPageParam: (last) => (last.length === PAGE_SIZE ? last[last.length - 1].createdAt : undefined),
> 287	  });
> 288	}
> 289	
> ```

> 1	import { useEffect } from 'react';
> 2	import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
> 3	import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
> 4	import { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';
> 5	
> 6	/**
> 7	 * vm_canvas_executions row shape relayed by the Realtime callback. Includes
> 8	 * the columns Phase 4.2c needs to reconcile the Zustand store on the client.
> 9	 */
> 10	export interface CanvasExecutionRow {
> 11	  id: string;
> 12	  project_id: string;
> 13	  node_id: string | null;
> 14	  status: 'pending' | 'queued' | 'running' | 'success' | 'failed' | 'cancelled' | 'timeout';
> 15	  output_url: string | null;
> 16	  error_message: string | null;
> 17	  completed_at: string | null;
> 18	}
> 19	
> 20	/**
> 21	 * Subscribes to vm_canvas_executions UPDATEs for the current user via Supabase Realtime.
> 22	 * Invalidates TanStack Query keys on every status transition AND invokes the optional
> 23	 * `onUpdate` callback so callers can reconcile the Zustand canvas store with the row.
> 24	 *
> 25	 * Used by Phase 4.2c async video flow: higgsfield-webhook flips status
> 26	 * `queued → success/failed/timeout`, this hook (a) refetches query caches and
> 27	 * (b) delegates the node-level status sync to the caller via `onUpdate`.
> 28	 *
> 29	 * Pattern mirrors `useMcorchMemory.ts:91-112` (canonical Realtime subscription).
> 30	 * RLS-aligned: `user_id=eq.<auth.uid>` filter ensures cross-tenant isolation at
> 31	 * the channel level. The callback is the single bridge between Realtime → Zustand,
> 32	 * keeping the store as the source of truth for node visual state.
> 33	 */
> 34	export function useCanvasJobsRealtime(
> 35	  projectId?: string,
> 36	  onUpdate?: (row: CanvasExecutionRow) => void,
> 37	): void {
> 38	  const qc = useQueryClient();
> 39	  const { user } = useAuth();
> 40	
> 41	  useEffect(() => {
> 42	    if (!user) return;
> 43	
> 44	    const channel = supabase
> 45	      .channel(`canvas_jobs_${user.id}`)
> 46	      .on(
> 47	        'postgres_changes',
> 48	        {
> 49	          event: 'UPDATE',
> 50	          schema: 'public',
> 51	          table: 'vm_canvas_executions',
> 52	          filter: `user_id=eq.${user.id}`,
> 53	        },
> 54	        (payload) => {
> 55	          const row = payload.new as CanvasExecutionRow;
> 56	          qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['canvas-execution', row.id] });
> 57	          if (projectId && row.project_id === projectId) {
> 58	            qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['vm-canvas-assets', projectId] });
> 59	            qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['vm-canvas-executions', projectId] });
> 60	          }
> 61	          onUpdate?.(row);
> 62	        },
> 63	      )
> 64	      .subscribe();
> 65	
> 66	    return () => {
> 67	      supabase.removeChannel(channel);
> 68	    };
> 69	  }, [user?.id, projectId, qc, onUpdate]);
> 70	}
> 71	
> ```
